from enum import Enum



class IntentType(Enum):

COMMAND = "COMMAND" # Actionable drone operation

CHAT = "CHAT" # Conversational input for OBI

CRITICAL = "CRITICAL" # Emergency interrupts (Kill switch)



class RomeProtocolParser:

def __init__(self):

# 1. Hard-coded Safety Triggers (The "Reflex" Layer)

self.critical_triggers = ["HALT", "STOP", "ABORT", "KILL", "DROP"]


# 2. Operational Command Dictionary (The "Syntax" Layer)

self.command_map = {

"LAUNCH": {"action": "takeoff", "altitude": 1.5},

"TAKEOFF": {"action": "takeoff", "altitude": 1.5},

"LAND": {"action": "land", "priority": "high"},

"RETURN": {"action": "rtl", "home_lock": True},

"HOME": {"action": "rtl", "home_lock": True},

"SCAN": {"action": "yaw_spin", "degrees": 360, "speed": 10},

"UP": {"action": "move", "direction": "up", "value": 1.0},

"DOWN": {"action": "move", "direction": "down", "value": 1.0}

}



def parse_input(self, user_input):

"""

Analyzes raw input to determine if it's a command or conversation.

Returns a JSON-ready dictionary.

"""

clean_input = user_input.strip().upper()



# CHECK 1: CRITICAL INTERRUPTS (Zero Latency check)

# If any emergency word is present, override everything.

for trigger in self.critical_triggers:

if trigger in clean_input:

return {

"type": IntentType.CRITICAL.value,

"payload": {"action": "EMERGENCY_STOP"},

"original_text": user_input,

"obi_response": "PROTOCOL OVERRIDE: EMERGENCY STOP EXECUTED."

}



# CHECK 2: OPERATIONAL COMMANDS

# Scans for specific keywords defined in the Rome Protocol.

# This allows natural phrasing like "Obi, launch the drone" -> Detects "LAUNCH"

detected_command = None

for keyword, instruction in self.command_map.items():

# Using word boundary \b to ensure "landing" doesn't trigger "land" if strict

if re.search(r'\b' + re.escape(keyword) + r'\b', clean_input):

detected_command = instruction

break # Execute first found command priority



if detected_command:

return {

"type": IntentType.COMMAND.value,

"payload": detected_command,

"original_text": user_input,

"obi_response": f"Copy. Executing {detected_command['action']}."

}



# CHECK 3: CONVERSATIONAL FALLBACK

# No commands found. Route to OBI LLM for personality response.

return {

"type": IntentType.CHAT.value,

"payload": None,

"original_text": user_input,

"obi_context": "Generate natural response based on persona."

}



# --- UNIT TEST ---

if __name__ == "__main__":

parser = RomeProtocolParser()



# Test Cases

test_inputs = [

"Obi, please launch the drone now.", # Natural language command

"ABORT MISSION IMMEDIATELY", # Emergency

"What is the battery status?", # Chat/Query

"Let's go home." # Natural language command

]



print("--- ROME PROTOCOL PARSER TEST ---\n")

for msg in test_inputs:

result = parser.parse_input(msg)

print(f"INPUT: '{msg}'")

print(f"ROUTING: {json.dumps(result, indent=2)}\n")

* Safety: The CRITICAL class overrides everything else.

# --- STEP 8: MAVLINK COMMAND MAPPING ---

def execute_hardware_command(action, params):

"""

Translates Agent 99 JSON into MAVLink packets.

"""

if not drone_state["connected"]:

return "ERROR: Drone Not Connected"



# 1. ARM MOTORS (The Ignition)

if action == "ARM":

master.mav.command_long_send(

master.target_system,

master.target_component,

mavutil.mavlink.MAV_CMD_COMPONENT_ARM_DISARM,

0,

1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0 # 1 = Arm, 0 = Disarm

)

return "ARMING_SEQUENCE_INITIATED"



# 2. TAKEOFF (The Launch)

elif action == "TAKEOFF":

altitude = float(params.get('alt', 1.5)) # Default 1.5m


# FAILSAFE: Switch to GUIDED mode first

# Custom mode 4 is usually GUIDED on Copter. Check your flight stack.

mode_id = master.mode_mapping()['GUIDED']

master.set_mode(mode_id)


# Send Takeoff Command

master.mav.command_long_send(

master.target_system,

master.target_component,

mavutil.mavlink.MAV_CMD_NAV_TAKEOFF,

0,

0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, altitude

)

return f"TAKEOFF_TO_{altitude}M"



# 3. LAND (The Return)

elif action == "LAND":

mode_id = master.mode_mapping()['LAND']

master.set_mode(mode_id)

return "LANDING_SEQUENCE_ACTIVE"



# 4. YAW/SCAN (The "Look Around")

elif action == "SCAN":

degrees = float(params.get('degrees', 360))

master.mav.command_long_send(

master.target_system,

master.target_component,

mavutil.mavlink.MAV_CMD_CONDITION_YAW,

0,

degrees, # Target angle (0-360)

10, # Speed (deg/sec)

1, # Direction (-1 ccw, 1 cw)

1, # Relative offset (1) or Absolute angle (0)

0, 0, 0

)

return "SCANNING_AREA"



# 5. VELOCITY CONTROL (Nudge Logic)

elif action == "MOVE":

# This handles "Go Left", "Go Forward"

# Requires SET_POSITION_TARGET_LOCAL_NED

pass # Placeholder for Step 9 (Advanced Movement)



return "UNKNOWN_COMMAND"



# --- UPDATE THE FLASK ROUTE ---

def receive_command():

data = request.json

action = data.get('action')

params = data.get('params', {})


print(f"[AGENT 99 SAYS]: {action}")


# Execute Real Hardware Command

result = execute_hardware_command(action, params)


return jsonify({"status": "ACK", "drone_response": result})

Step 7 Implementation: Real-Time MAVLink Telemetry

Action: Update your local drone_link.py with this code.

import time

import threading

from flask import Flask, jsonify, request

from pymavlink import mavutil



# --- CONFIGURATION ---

# Replace with your actual connection string

# Linux/Pi USB: '/dev/ttyACM0' or '/dev/ttyAMA0'

# Windows: 'COM3' or 'COM4'

CONNECTION_STRING = '/dev/ttyACM0'

BAUD_RATE = 57600



app = Flask(__name__)



# --- GLOBAL STATE (The "Heartbeat" Storage) ---

# This dictionary is constantly updated by the background thread.

drone_state = {

"connected": False,

"mode": "UNKNOWN",

"armed": False,

"battery_voltage": 0.0,

"battery_remaining": 0,

"altitude_relative": 0.0,

"heading": 0,

"last_update": 0

}



# --- BACKGROUND WORKER: THE LISTENER ---

def mavlink_loop():

print(f"[ROME PROTOCOL] Attempting connection to {CONNECTION_STRING}...")

try:

# Establish MAVLink connection

master = mavutil.mavlink_connection(CONNECTION_STRING, baud=BAUD_RATE)


# Wait for the first heartbeat (this confirms hardware is alive)

master.wait_heartbeat()

print(f"[ROME PROTOCOL] HARDWARE LOCK CONFIRMED. System Online.")

drone_state["connected"] = True



while True:

# Listen for specific data packets

msg = master.recv_match(

type=['SYS_STATUS', 'GLOBAL_POSITION_INT', 'HEARTBEAT'],

blocking=True,

timeout=1.0

)


if not msg:

continue



# Update Global State

drone_state["last_update"] = time.time()


if msg.get_type() == 'HEARTBEAT':

drone_state["mode"] = mavutil.mode_string_v10(msg)

drone_state["armed"] = bool(msg.base_mode & mavutil.mavlink.MAV_MODE_FLAG_SAFETY_ARMED)


if msg.get_type() == 'SYS_STATUS':

drone_state["battery_voltage"] = msg.voltage_battery / 1000.0 # mV to V

drone_state["battery_remaining"] = msg.battery_remaining # Percentage



if msg.get_type() == 'GLOBAL_POSITION_INT':

drone_state["altitude_relative"] = msg.relative_alt / 1000.0 # mm to m

drone_state["heading"] = msg.hdg / 100.0 # cdeg to deg



except Exception as e:

print(f"[CRITICAL ERROR] MAVLink Loop Failed: {e}")

drone_state["connected"] = False



# --- START THE HEARTBEAT THREAD ---

# Daemon=True ensures this thread dies when the main app stops.

t = threading.Thread(target=mavlink_loop, daemon=True)

t.start()



# --- AGENT 99 ENDPOINT ---

@app.route('/status', methods=['GET'])

def get_status():

"""

Agent 99 calls this endpoint via the Tunnel to get the current pulse.

"""

# Safety Check: Is data stale? (e.g., connection lost)

if time.time() - drone_state["last_update"] > 5.0:

drone_state["connected"] = False



return jsonify(drone_state)



# --- COMMAND ENDPOINT (From Step 6) ---

@app.route('/command', methods=['POST'])

def receive_command():

data = request.json

action = data.get('action')

print(f"[COMMAND RECEIVED] {action}")

return jsonify({"status": "ACK", "action": action})



if __name__ == '__main__':

# Run the Flask server

app.run(port=5000, debug=False)

# --- STEP 8: THE COMMAND EXECUTOR ---

def execute_hardware_command(master, action, params):
    """
    Translates Agent 99 JSON into physical MAVLink instructions.
    """
    if not drone_state["connected"]:
        return "CRITICAL: HARDWARE DISCONNECTED"

    # 8.1 & 8.2: ARM MOTORS & GUIDED MODE ENFORCEMENT
    # Most flight stacks require GUIDED mode to accept high-level commands.
    if action == "ARM":
        # First, force GUIDED mode
        mode_id = master.mode_mapping()['GUIDED']
        master.set_mode(mode_id)
        
        # Then, send the ARM command
        master.mav.command_long_send(
            master.target_system,
            master.target_component,
            mavutil.mavlink.MAV_CMD_COMPONENT_ARM_DISARM,
            0,
            1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0 # 1 to Arm
        )
        return "ARMING_SEQUENCE_INITIATED_IN_GUIDED_MODE"

    # 8.3: TAKEOFF (The "Launch" Command)
    elif action == "TAKEOFF":
        target_alt = float(params.get('altitude', 1.5)) # Default 1.5m if not specified
        
        master.mav.command_long_send(
            master.target_system,
            master.target_component,
            mavutil.mavlink.MAV_CMD_NAV_TAKEOFF,
            0,
            0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, target_alt
        )
        return f"LAUNCH_SEQUENCE_EXECUTING_TARGET_{target_alt}M"

    # 8.4: LAND / RTL (The "Go Home" Command)
    elif action == "LAND":
        mode_id = master.mode_mapping()['LAND']
        master.set_mode(mode_id)
        return "EXECUTING_PRECISION_LANDING"

    elif action == "RTL" or action == "HOME":
        mode_id = master.mode_mapping()['RTL']
        master.set_mode(mode_id)
        return "RETURNING_TO_SOVEREIGN_BASE"

    return "COMMAND_UNKNOWN_IN_ROME_PROTOCOL"

# --- UPDATE YOUR RECEIVE_COMMAND ROUTE ---

def receive_command():
    data = request.json
    action = data.get('action', '').upper()
    params = data.get('params', {})
    
    # Access the 'master' object from your mavlink_loop
    # Note: In a production script, you'd want to make 'master' accessible globally
    global master_connection 
    
    result = execute_hardware_command(master_connection, action, params)
    
    return jsonify({
        "status": "ACK",
        "agent_99_relay": result,
        "timestamp": time.time()
    })

Step 9 Implementation: The Smooth Operator

Action: Update drone_link.py.

We are moving from "Synchronous Execution" (waiting for the drone to finish before replying) to "Asynchronous Execution" (Instant Reply + Background Processing). This prevents the web server from freezing if the hardware is busy.

1. The Code Update (Async Queue + Socket Tweak):

import queue

import socket



# --- STEP 9.3: THE JITTER BUFFER (COMMAND QUEUE) ---

# Commands go here instantly so the Web Server can reply "ACK" immediately.

command_queue = queue.Queue()



# --- STEP 9.2: SOCKET OPTIMIZATION (DISABLE NAGLE) ---

# Force small packets to send immediately rather than waiting to bundle.

# (This is usually handled by Flask/Gunicorn, but we enforce it on the MAVLink socket if using TCP)

if 'tcp' in CONNECTION_STRING:

# Example for a TCP MAVLink connection

sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)

sock.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_TCP, socket.TCP_NODELAY, 1)



# --- BACKGROUND WORKER: THE EXECUTOR ---

def command_worker():

print("[ROME PROTOCOL] Command Worker Started. Waiting for orders...")

while True:

try:

# Block until a command arrives.

# This is the "Buffer" - it handles bursts of commands smoothly.

item = command_queue.get()


action = item['action']

params = item['params']


print(f"[EXECUTING] {action} (Latency: {time.time() - item['timestamp']:.3f}s)")


# Execute the hardware logic from Step 8

result = execute_hardware_command(action, params)


command_queue.task_done()


except Exception as e:

print(f"[WORKER ERROR] {e}")



# Start the Worker Thread

t_worker = threading.Thread(target=command_worker, daemon=True)

t_worker.start()



# --- UPDATED FLASK ROUTE (ZERO LATENCY) ---

@app.route('/command', methods=['POST'])

def receive_command():

data = request.json


# Push to Queue with Timestamp (for latency tracking)

cmd_packet = {

'action': data.get('action'),

'params': data.get('params', {}),

'timestamp': time.time()

}


command_queue.put(cmd_packet)


# Return immediately! Don't wait for the drone.

# The UI gets an instant "sent" feedback.

return jsonify({"status": "QUEUED", "msg": "Command in Buffer"})

    <script>
        // 10.1: TELEMETRY POLLING (The Heartbeat)
        function updateStats() {
            fetch('/status')
                .then(response => response.json())
                .then(data => {
                    document.getElementById('alt').innerText = data.altitude_relative.toFixed(1);
                    document.getElementById('bat').innerText = data.battery_remaining;
                    document.getElementById('mode').innerText = data.mode;
                    const status = document.getElementById('connection-status');
                    if(data.connected) {
                        status.innerText = "HARDWARE: LOCK CONFIRMED";
                        status.className = "status-connected";
                    } else {
                        status.innerText = "HARDWARE: LINK LOST";
                        status.className = "status-disconnected";
                    }
                });
        }
        setInterval(updateStats, 500); // 2Hz refresh rate for UI

        // 10.2: COMMAND DISPATCH
        function sendCommand() {
            const input = document.getElementById('cmd-input');
            const chat = document.getElementById('chat-window');
            const msg = input.value;
            
            chat.innerHTML += `<div><span style="color: #fff;">Agent 100:</span> ${msg}</div>`;
            
            fetch('/command', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ action: msg }) // This goes to the Rome Protocol Parser
            })
            .then(res => res.json())
            .then(data => {
                chat.innerHTML += `<div><span style="color: #00ff41;">OBI:</span> ${data.agent_99_relay}</div>`;
                chat.scrollTop = chat.scrollHeight;
            });

            input.value = '';
        }

        function sendKill() {
            document.getElementById('cmd-input').value = "KILL";
            sendCommand();
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime

# --- STEP 11: THE LEDGER INITIALIZATION ---
def init_ledger():
    conn = sqlite3.connect('mission_ledger.db')
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    # Create the table for both Commands and Telemetry Snaps
    cursor.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ledger (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            timestamp DATETIME,
            type TEXT, -- 'COMMAND' or 'TELEMETRY'
            action TEXT,
            data TEXT, -- JSON string of params or state
            sovereign_voice TEXT -- The original English input
        )
    ''')
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

def log_to_ledger(entry_type, action, data, voice=""):
    conn = sqlite3.connect('mission_ledger.db')
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute('''
        INSERT INTO ledger (timestamp, type, action, data, sovereign_voice)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    ''', (datetime.now(), entry_type, action, str(data), voice))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Initialize the DB on startup
init_ledger()

Step 19: The Iron Seal (Sovereign Handshake)

We are implementing a Time-Based HMAC (Hash-based Message Authentication Code). This prevents "Replay Attacks." Even if someone intercepts your command, it becomes useless 30 seconds later.

19.1: The Sovereign Key Generation (Agent 99 Cloud)

In your Cloudflare Worker, we add the "Validator."

// Agent 99 Validator Logic

async function verifySovereignWill(clientSignature, timestamp, message) {

const secret = env.SOVEREIGN_SECRET;

const dataToSign = `${timestamp}:${message}`;


// Generate the server's version of the signature

const expectedSignature = await HMAC_SHA256(secret, dataToSign);


// FAIL CLOSED: If the time is more than 30s off, or signature mismatches, lock down.

const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

if (Math.abs(now - timestamp) > 30) return false;


return clientSignature === expectedSignature;

}

Step 19.3: The Sovereign Signing Code (Frontend)
To initiate the Step 20 Benchmark, your Throne Room (the frontend) must be able to sign its own "Will." Add this cryptographic function to your throne.html (or the script handling the dispatch) to generate the HMAC signature using a shared secret.

JavaScript
// 19.3: The Sovereign Handshake Generator
async function signSovereignWill(message, secret) {
    const timestamp = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    const dataToSign = `${timestamp}:${message}`;
    
    // Using SubtleCrypto for high-performance browser-side signing
    const encoder = new TextEncoder();
    const keyData = encoder.encode(secret);
    const msgData = encoder.encode(dataToSign);

    const cryptoKey = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
        'raw', keyData, { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' },
        false, ['sign']
    );

    const signatureBuffer = await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', cryptoKey, msgData);
    const signatureArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(signatureBuffer));
    const signatureHex = signatureArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');

    return { signature: signatureHex, timestamp: timestamp };
}

import random

import math



def calculate_random_waypoint(current_lat, current_lon, radius_meters=10):

"""

Step 18.1: Defines a random coordinate within a safe 'bubble'.

"""

# Convert radius from meters to degrees (approximate)

radius_in_degrees = radius_meters / 111320.0



u = random.random()

v = random.random()

w = radius_in_degrees * math.sqrt(u)

t = 2 * math.pi * v

x = w * math.cos(t)

y = w * math.sin(t)



# Adjust for the latitude shrinkage

new_lat = current_lat + x

new_lon = current_lon + y / math.cos(math.radians(current_lat))


return new_lat, new_lon



def start_random_mission():

"""

Step 18.3 & 18.4: The 'Sovereign' mission loop.

"""

if not drone_state["connected"] or not drone_state["armed"]:

return "DENIED: System must be Armed and Flying for mission start."



# 1. Pick a Target

target_lat, target_lon = calculate_random_waypoint(

drone_state.get('lat', 10.7626), # Default to HCMC coord

drone_state.get('lon', 106.6601)

)



# 2. Command the Flight Controller

# master.mav.send(mavutil.mavlink.MAVLink_set_position_target_global_int_message(...))


print(f"[MISSION] Obi chose coordinates: {target_lat}, {target_lon}")


# 3. Log to Ledger (The 'Work')

write_to_ledger("Discovery Mission", "RANDOM_WALK", {"lat": target_lat, "lon": target_lon})


return f"MISSION_STARTED: Exploring coordinate offset {target_lat:.5f}, {target_lon:.5f}"

import hmac
import hashlib

# THE SOVEREIGN SECRET (Keep this in your .env or memory only)
SOVEREIGN_SECRET = b"PHOENIX_OMEGA_2026_SOVEREIGN" 

def verify_sovereign_will(message, client_signature, timestamp):
    """
    Step 19.1: The Cryptographic Gatekeeper.
    """
    # 1. Check for 'Stale' commands (TTL: 30 seconds)
    if abs(time.time() - timestamp) > 30:
        return False, "ERROR: COMMAND_STALE_EXPIRED"

    # 2. Reconstruct the hash
    data_to_sign = f"{timestamp}:{message}".encode()
    expected_signature = hmac.new(SOVEREIGN_SECRET, data_to_sign, hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()

    # 3. Compare (Constant time comparison to prevent timing attacks)
    if hmac.compare_digest(expected_signature, client_signature):
        return True, "WILL_VERIFIED"
    
    return False, "DENIED: INVALID_SOVEREIGN_SIGNATURE"

/**

* STAGE 19.1: THE SOVEREIGN SIGNATURE

* Generates a time-sensitive HMAC signature for every command.

*/

async function signSovereignCommand(message, secret) {

const timestamp = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

const dataToSign = `${timestamp}:${message}`;


const encoder = new TextEncoder();

const keyData = encoder.encode(secret);

const msgData = encoder.encode(dataToSign);



const cryptoKey = await crypto.subtle.importKey(

'raw', keyData, { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign']

);


const signatureBuffer = await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', cryptoKey, msgData);

const signatureArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(signatureBuffer));

const signatureHex = signatureArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');



return { signature: signatureHex, timestamp: timestamp };

}



// Update the sendToObi() function to include the signature:

const auth = await signSovereignCommand(text, SOVEREIGN_SECRET);

const response = await fetch(CLOUDFLARE_WORKER_URL, {

method: 'POST',

headers: {

'Content-Type': 'application/json',

'X-Sovereign-Sig': auth.signature,

'X-Sovereign-Time': auth.timestamp

},

body: JSON.stringify({ message: text })

});

# --- STEP 22: THE LEDGER FEED ---

@app.route('/ledger-feed', methods=['GET'])
def get_ledger_feed():
    """
    Agent 98 provides the latest mission logs for the UI.
    """
    conn = sqlite3.connect('mission_ledger.db')
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    # Fetch the last 15 entries to show the 'work' happening
    cursor.execute('SELECT timestamp, type, action, data FROM ledger ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 15')
    rows = cursor.fetchall()
    conn.close()

    feed = [{"time": r[0], "type": r[1], "action": r[2], "data": r[3]} for r in rows]
    return jsonify(feed)

import time

import json

from flask import Flask, request, jsonify

from pymavlink import mavutil



# --- MONOLITHIC CONFIGURATION ---

CONNECTION_STRING = '/dev/ttyACM0'

BAUD_RATE = 57600

app = Flask(__name__)



# --- THE PHYSICAL LINK ---

print("[REINCARNATION] Awakening Hardware Link...")

master = mavutil.mavlink_connection(CONNECTION_STRING, baud=BAUD_RATE)

master.wait_heartbeat()

print("[REINCARNATION] Hardware Heartbeat Detected. Body and Soul Unified.")



@app.route('/command', methods=['POST'])

def execute_monolith_will():

"""

The Zero-Gap Downlink.

Receives the signed .js intent and translates to MAVLink in microseconds.

"""

start_time = time.time()

data = request.json

action = data.get('action')

params = data.get('params', {})



# STAGE 26: THE REFLEXES

if action == "TAKEOFF":

# GUIDED MODE + ARM + TAKEOFF in one burst

master.set_mode(master.mode_mapping()['GUIDED'])

master.arducopter_arm()

master.mav.command_long_send(

master.target_system, master.target_component,

mavutil.mavlink.MAV_CMD_NAV_TAKEOFF, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, params.get('alt', 2.5)

)

msg = "ASCENDING"



elif action == "SCAN":

master.mav.command_long_send(

master.target_system, master.target_component,

mavutil.mavlink.MAV_CMD_CONDITION_YAW, 0, 360, 15, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0

)

msg = "SCANNING"



elif action == "RTL":

master.set_mode(master.mode_mapping()['RTL'])

msg = "RETURNING_HOME"



else:

msg = "IDLE_AWAITING_WILL"



execution_latency = f"{(time.time() - start_time) * 1000:.2f}ms"


# Return directly to the .js Monolith

return jsonify({

"status": "EXECUTED",

"reflex_latency": execution_latency,

"message": msg,

"ledger_id": data.get('reincarnation_id')

})



if __name__ == '__main__':

# Force the server to be as fast as possible

app.run(port=5000, threaded=True, debug=False)

import random
import math

# --- STEP 18.2: SOVEREIGN DISCOVERY ENGINE ---

def generate_discovery_waypoint(home_lat, home_lon, max_radius=10):
    """
    Calculates a random, safe coordinate within the 10m Studio Bubble.
    """
    # 1. Generate random distance and angle
    radius = max_radius * math.sqrt(random.random())
    angle = 2 * math.pi * random.random()

    # 2. Offset coordinates (Simplified for small-scale studio movement)
    # 1 meter is roughly 0.000009 degrees
    lat_offset = (radius * math.cos(angle)) * 0.000009
    lon_offset = (radius * math.sin(angle)) * 0.000009

    target_lat = home_lat + lat_offset
    target_lon = home_lon + lon_offset

    return target_lat, target_lon

def execute_random_walk():
    """
    The 'Initiate and Build' command execution.
    """
    # 1. Get current 'Home' from the Ledger or GPS
    home_lat = drone_state.get('lat', 10.762)
    home_lon = drone_state.get('lon', 106.660)

    # 2. Calculate the next Discovery Point
    t_lat, t_lon = generate_discovery_waypoint(home_lat, home_lon)

    # 3. Log the 'Intent to Explore' to Agent 98
    log_id = log_to_ledger("DISCOVERY", "NEW_WAYPOINT", {"lat": t_lat, "lon": t_lon})

    # 4. Command the Hardware (The Reflex)
    # We use the GUIDED waypoint command
    master.mav.send(mavutil.mavlink.MAVLink_set_position_target_global_int_message(
        10, master.target_system, master.target_component,
        mavutil.mavlink.MAV_FRAME_GLOBAL_RELATIVE_ALT,
        0b110111111000, # Position only mask
        int(t_lat * 1e7), int(t_lon * 1e7), 2.5, # Target Alt 2.5m
        0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
    ))

    return f"REINCARNATION_ID_{log_id}: Navigating to new discovery quadrant."

// --- AGENT 99: SOVEREIGN VALIDATOR GATE ---

async function handleCommandRequest(request, env) {

const { action, timestamp, signature, params } = await request.json();

const secret = env.SOVEREIGN_SECRET; // Set this in your Cloudflare Dashboard



// 1. GENERATE SERVER-SIDE HASH

const encoder = new TextEncoder();

const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(

'raw', encoder.encode(secret),

{ name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign']

);


const dataToSign = encoder.encode(`${timestamp}:${action}`);

const serverSigBuffer = await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', key, dataToSign);

const serverSignature = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(serverSigBuffer)));



// 2. THE FAIL-CLOSED CHECK

const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

const isTimedOut = Math.abs(now - timestamp) > 30;


if (signature !== serverSignature || isTimedOut) {

return new Response(JSON.stringify({

status: "REJECTED",

error: "UNAUTHORIZED_WILL_OR_DRIFT"

}), { status: 403 });

}



// 3. AUTHORIZED: PROCEED TO HARDWARE DOWNLINK

return await relayToDrone(action, params);

}

// --- STEP 19.1: THE SOVEREIGN VALIDATOR ---

async function validateSovereignSignature(request, env) {

const { signature, timestamp, action } = await request.json();

const secret = env.SOVEREIGN_SECRET; // Set this in your Cloudflare Env


// 1. Time-Drift Check (Anti-Replay)

const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

if (Math.abs(now - timestamp) > 30) {

throw new Error("REJECTED: COMMAND_STALE_OR_DRIFT");

}



// 2. Signature Verification

const dataToSign = `${timestamp}:${action}`;

const hmac = await crypto.subtle.importKey(

"raw", new TextEncoder().encode(secret),

{ name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" }, false, ["verify"]

);


const isValid = await crypto.subtle.verify(

"HMAC", hmac, hexToUint8Array(signature), new TextEncoder().encode(dataToSign)

);



if (!isValid) throw new Error("REJECTED: UNAUTHORIZED_WILL");

return true;

}

JavaScript
// --- STEP 19.2: THE HANDSHAKE GENERATOR ---
async function generateHMAC(secret, data) {
    const encoder = new TextEncoder();
    const keyData = encoder.encode(secret);
    const msgData = encoder.encode(data);

    const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
        'raw', keyData, { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' },
        false, ['sign']
    );

    const sigBuffer = await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', key, msgData);
    return Array.from(new Uint8Array(sigBuffer))
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
        .join('');
}

async function signAndExecute(actionText) {
    const timestamp = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    const secret = "PHOENIX_OMEGA_2026_SOVEREIGN"; // MUST match Cloudflare ENV
    
    // Sign the Intent
    const signature = await generateHMAC(secret, `${timestamp}:${actionText}`);
    
    // 10.3: The Command Bridge
    const response = await fetch('/command', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ 
            action: actionText, 
            timestamp: timestamp, 
            signature: signature 
        })
    });
    
    return await response.json();
}

return { action: "CHAT", params: { text: input } };

}

/**

export default {

async fetch(request, env) {

const start = performance.now();

const { action, params, sovereign_sig, timestamp } = await request.json();



// STEP 19: THE IRON SEAL (IMPROVEMENT 2)

if (!await isValidSovereignWill(sovereign_sig, timestamp, action, env.SOVEREIGN_SECRET)) {

return new Response("UNAUTHORIZED", { status: 403 });

}



// STEP 25: THE FUSION MEMORY (IMPROVEMENT 3)

const id = env.SOVEREIGN_STATE.idFromName("GENESIS_NODE");

const monolith = env.SOVEREIGN_STATE.get(id);


// STEP 24: THE LEGION IDENTIFIER

const droneID = params.drone_id || "PHOENIX_01";



// TWO-PHASE COMMIT (IMPROVEMENT 9)

// STEP 8: THE KINETIC DOWNLINK (IMPROVEMENT 5)

const droneResponse = await fetch(`${env.TUNNEL_URL}/command`, {

method: 'POST',

headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },

body: JSON.stringify({ action, params, ledger_height: record.height })

});



return new Response(JSON.stringify({

obi: `Reincarnated. ${droneID} is responsive.`,

stone_record: `Height ${record.height}`,

latency: `${(performance.now() - start).toFixed(2)}ms`

}));

}

};

"use strict";



/**

* PHOENIX OMEGA FUSION MONOLITH v1.0

import { sha256Hex, timingSafeEq, nowISO, mustInt } from "./p10/03_crypto.js"; // Standardized helpers



export default {

async fetch(request, env) {

const url = new URL(request.url);

const authKey = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key");



// --- STAGE 6.1: THE AUTHORITY CHAIN ---

const isSovereign = timingSafeEq(authKey, env.SOVEREIGN_KEY);



// Public Health & Pulse

if (url.pathname === "/pulse") {

return Response.json({ status: "HEARTBEAT_ACTIVE", node: "HCMC_01", version: "v100.6" });

}



// --- STAGE 6.2 & 9.1: KINETIC INTENT GATEWAY ---

if (url.pathname === "/execute" && request.method === "POST") {

if (!isSovereign) return new Response("ACCESS_DENIED", { status: 401 });



const payload = await request.json();

const kineticKeywords = ["RISE", "HALT", "SCAN", "REINCARNATE"];

const isKinetic = kineticKeywords.includes(payload.action?.toUpperCase());



// If kinetic, we trigger the Recursive Feedback Loop (6.5)

if (isKinetic) {

const ledgerId = env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME");

const ledger = env.LEDGER.get(ledgerId);


// Commit Intent to Stone BEFORE motor pulse

const appendRes = await ledger.fetch(new Request("https://ledger/ledger/append", {

method: "POST",

body: JSON.stringify({ ...payload, type: "KINETIC_WILL" })

}));

const commit = await appendRes.json();



// P10/05: Recursive Confirmation

if (commit.ok) {

// Downlink to Local Hardware (reincarnate_body.py)

return Response.json({

obi: `Decree Sealed at Height ${commit.height}. Executing ${payload.action}.`,

hash: commit.hash,

execute: true

});

}

}

}



// Fallback to Router (src/p10/08_routes.js)

return env.ROUTER.fetch(request, env);

}

};



// --- STAGE 7.1: THE MERKLE LEDGER DO ---

export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 {

constructor(state, env) {

this.state = state;

this.storage = state.storage;

this.sql = state.storage.sql;

}



async fetch(request) {

const url = new URL(request.url);


if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append") {

const data = await request.json();

const height = (await this.storage.get("height")) || 0;

const prevHash = (await this.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS";

const nextHeight = height + 1;


// STEP 13.1: SHA-256 Binary Packing

const ts = nowISO();

const hash = await sha256Hex(`${nextHeight}:${prevHash}:${ts}:${JSON.stringify(data)}`);


// Commit to SQLite (The Stone Record)

this.sql.exec(

"INSERT INTO ledger (height, hash, prev_hash, payload, ts) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",

nextHeight, hash, prevHash, JSON.stringify(data), ts

);



await this.storage.put("height", nextHeight);

await this.storage.put("latest_hash", hash);



return Response.json({ ok: true, height: nextHeight, hash });

}

}

}

"use strict";



/**

 * PHOENIX_5IN1_KERNEL_V9_AUTHORITY_CHAIN

 * ENHANCEMENTS: UUID enforcement, semantic filter thresholds, Agent 98 definition, custody logging

 */



const crypto = require("crypto");



function canonicalJSON(v) {

  if (v === null) return "null";

  if (typeof v === "boolean") return v ? "true" : "false";

  if (typeof v === "number") {

    if (!Number.isFinite(v)) throw new Error("NON_FINITE_NUMBER");

    return JSON.stringify(v);

  }

  if (typeof v === "string") return JSON.stringify(v);

  if (Array.isArray(v)) return "[" + v.map(canonicalJSON).join(",") + "]";

  if (typeof v === "object") {

    const keys = Object.keys(v).sort();

    return "{" + keys.map(k => JSON.stringify(k) + ":" + canonicalJSON(v[k])).join(",") + "}";

  }

  throw new Error("UNSUPPORTED_TYPE");

}



function sha256HexUtf8(s) {

  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(Buffer.from(String(s), "utf8")).digest("hex");

}



// D027 FIX: Sovereign UUID enforcement

class SovereignAuthority {

  constructor(uuid) {

    if (!uuid || !/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(uuid)) {

      throw new Error("INVALID_SOVEREIGN_UUID_FORMAT");

    }

    this.uuid = uuid;

    this.override_priority = Infinity; // Absolute priority

  }



  validateCommand(command) {

    if (!command.sovereign_uuid || command.sovereign_uuid !== this.uuid) {

      throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED_COMMAND_SOVEREIGN_UUID_MISMATCH");

    }

    return true;

  }

}



// D028 FIX: Zero-Gap threshold logic

class SemanticFilter {

  constructor() {

    this.confidence_threshold = 0.85;

    this.execution_keywords = ["execute", "do it", "go", "now", "immediately"];

  }



  shouldExecute(userInput, intentConfidence, conversationHistory) {

    // Threshold 1: High confidence

    if (intentConfidence >= this.confidence_threshold) {

      return { execute: true, reason: "HIGH_CONFIDENCE" };

    }



    // Threshold 2: Explicit execution keyword

    const hasKeyword = this.execution_keywords.some(kw => 

      userInput.toLowerCase().includes(kw)

    );

    if (hasKeyword) {

      return { execute: true, reason: "EXECUTION_KEYWORD_DETECTED" };

    }



    // Threshold 3: Repeated similar command

    const lastCommand = conversationHistory[conversationHistory.length - 1];

    if (lastCommand && this.similarity(userInput, lastCommand.text) > 0.8) {

      return { execute: true, reason: "REPEATED_COMMAND" };

    }



    // Default: Ask for clarification

    return { execute: false, reason: "NEEDS_CLARIFICATION" };

  }



  similarity(str1, str2) {

    // Simplified Levenshtein distance ratio

    const longer = str1.length > str2.length ? str1 : str2;

    const shorter = str1.length > str2.length ? str2 : str1;

    if (longer.length === 0) return 1.0;

    

    const editDistance = this.levenshtein(str1.toLowerCase(), str2.toLowerCase());

    return (longer.length - editDistance) / longer.length;

  }



  levenshtein(a, b) {

    const matrix = [];

    for (let i = 0; i <= b.length; i++) {

      matrix[i] = [i];

    }

    for (let j = 0; j <= a.length; j++) {

      matrix[0][j] = j;

    }

    for (let i = 1; i <= b.length; i++) {

      for (let j = 1; j <= a.length; j++) {

        if (b.charAt(i - 1) === a.charAt(j - 1)) {

          matrix[i][j] = matrix[i - 1][j - 1];

        } else {

          matrix[i][j] = Math.min(

            matrix[i - 1][j - 1] + 1,

            matrix[i][j - 1] + 1,

            matrix[i - 1][j] + 1

          );

        }

      }

    }

    return matrix[b.length][a.length];

  }

async function sealCommand(text, uuid) {

    timestamp: ts,
    command_text: text,

// D031 FIX: Chain-of-custody logging format

class ChainOfCustodyLogger {

  constructor(sovereignUUID) {

    this.sovereignUUID = sovereignUUID;

  }



  createEntry(commandText) {

    const command_id = crypto.randomUUID();

    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString(); // ISO-8601 UTC with ms

    

    // D031 spec: SHA-256(timestamp + command_text + sovereign_uuid)

    const hash = sha256HexUtf8(`${timestamp}${commandText}${this.sovereignUUID}`);



    return Object.freeze({

      command_id,

      timestamp,

      command_text: commandText,

      sovereign_uuid: this.sovereignUUID,

      hash,

      status: "LOGGED"

    });

  }



  verifyEntry(entry) {

    const recomputed = sha256HexUtf8(

      `${entry.timestamp}${entry.command_text}${entry.sovereign_uuid}`

    );

    return recomputed === entry.hash;

  }

}



// D030 FIX: Recursive feedback loop with termination

class RecursiveFeedbackLoop {

  constructor(maxAttempts = 10, intervalMs = 500) {

    this.maxAttempts = maxAttempts;

    this.intervalMs = intervalMs;

  }



  async syncUntilConverged(getLedgerState, getPhysicalState, tolerance) {

    for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= this.maxAttempts; attempt++) {

      const ledgerState = await getLedgerState();

      const physicalState = await getPhysicalState();



      const divergence = this.calculateDivergence(ledgerState, physicalState);

      

      if (divergence <= tolerance) {

        return {

          converged: true,

          attempts: attempt,

          final_divergence: divergence

        };

      }



      console.log(`[FEEDBACK] Attempt ${attempt}/${this.maxAttempts}: divergence=${divergence}`);

      await this.sleep(this.intervalMs);

    }



    // D030 fix: Escalate instead of infinite loop

    throw new Error(

      `SYNC_FAILED: Could not converge after ${this.maxAttempts} attempts. ` +

      `Manual intervention required.`

    );

  }



  calculateDivergence(ledgerState, physicalState) {

    // Simplified: Real implementation would compare multiple dimensions

    return Math.abs(ledgerState.altitude - physicalState.altitude);

  }



  sleep(ms) {

    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

  }

}



// Ledger (unchanged structure)

class PhoenixAuthorityChainLedger {

  constructor({ genesisHead, sovereignUUID }) {

    this.head = genesisHead || "0".repeat(64);

    this.height = 0;

    this.events = [];

    this.authority = new SovereignAuthority(sovereignUUID);

    this.custodyLogger = new ChainOfCustodyLogger(sovereignUUID);

  }



  appendEvent(evt) {

    // Validate Sovereign authority

    if (evt.sovereign_uuid) {

      this.authority.validateCommand(evt);

    }



    const canon = canonicalJSON(evt);

    const hash = sha256HexUtf8(canon);

    const stored = Object.freeze({ ...evt, hash });

    this.events.push(stored);

    this.head = hash;

    this.height += 1;

    return stored;

  }



  verifyLocal() {

    for (let i = 0; i < this.events.length; i++) {

      const e = this.events[i];

      if (i > 0 && e.prev_hash !== this.events[i - 1].hash) {

        return { ok: false, reason: "CHAIN_BREAK", at: e.event_id };

      }

      const { hash, ...evt } = e;

      const recomputed = sha256HexUtf8(canonicalJSON(evt));

      if (recomputed !== hash) {

        return { ok: false, reason: "HASH_MISMATCH", at: e.event_id };

      }

    }

    return { ok: true, head: this.head, height: this.height };

  }

}

      status: "OPEN"

    });

  }



  // D031: Chain-of-custody format

  if (raw_text.includes("timestamped and hashed") && !raw_text.includes("SHA-256")) {

    out.push({

      defect_id: "D031",

      severity: "P1",

      type: "CHAIN_OF_CUSTODY_FORMAT",

      statement: "Custody logging requires hash but format unspecified.",

      evidence: [{ event_id, quote: "timestamped and hashed into Stone Record" }],

      impact: "Inconsistent ledger formats → verification breaks.",

      hardening_directive: "Specify: SHA-256(timestamp + command + uuid); ISO-8601 timestamps.",

      acceptance_test: "All entries follow schema; hash verification passes.",

      status: "OPEN"

    });

  }



  return out;

}



function buildSeal({ ledger, defects }) {

  const v = ledger.verifyLocal();

  if (!v.ok) {

    return Object.freeze({

      replay_status: "FAIL",

      fail_reason: v.reason,

      at: v.at

    });

  }

  return Object.freeze({

    kernel: "PHOENIX_5IN1_KERNEL_V9_AUTHORITY_CHAIN",

    head_hash: ledger.head,

    height: ledger.height,

    authority_chain: {

      "6.1_sovereign_override": "UUID enforcement implemented",

      "6.2_semantic_filter": "Confidence threshold + keyword detection",

      "6.3_agent_98": "Background task delegation defined",

      "6.4_custody_logging": "SHA-256 + ISO-8601 format spec",

      "6.5_feedback_loop": "Max attempts with escalation"

    },

    defects_hash: sha256HexUtf8(canonicalJSON(defects)),

    fixes_applied: ["D027_uuid_enforcement", "D028_threshold_logic", "D029_agent98_spec", "D030_loop_termination", "D031_custody_format"],

    replay_status: "PASS_LOCAL"

  });

}

validate(text) {

}

};

/**

* 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA — TIGER ENGINE V1.0

* AUTHORITY: AGENT-100

* MISSION: QUANTIFY CONFIDENCE. KILL THE MOUSE.

*/



const TIGER_ENGINE = {

// 🛑 THE MOUSE TRAP (Hesitation & Apology)

MOUSE_MARKERS: [

"maybe", "i think", "um", "uh", "sorry", "excuse me",

"little bit", "kind of", "actually", "just"

],



// ⚡ THE TIGER STRIKE (Assertion & Action)

TIGER_MARKERS: [

"will", "must", "done", "execute", "plan", "solve",

"ship", "deploy", "zero", "now"

],



/**

* Scores a transcript based on Confidence > Grammar.

* @param {string} text - The raw student input.

* @param {number} velocity - Words per minute (measured by frontend).

*/

evaluate(text, velocity = 0) {

const lower = text.toLowerCase();

let score = 50; // Start at Neutral



// 1. PENALIZE MOUSE VOICE

this.MOUSE_MARKERS.forEach(word => {

if (lower.includes(word)) score -= 10;

});



// 2. REWARD TIGER VOICE

this.TIGER_MARKERS.forEach(word => {

if (lower.includes(word)) score += 15;

});



// 3. VELOCITY MULTIPLIER (Speed = Confidence)

if (velocity > 120) score += 10; // Fast

if (velocity < 60) score -= 10; // Slow/Hesitant



// 4. CLAMP & CLASSIFY

score = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score));

return {

score: score,

tier: this.classify(score),

feedback: this.generateFeedback(score)

};

},



classify(score) {

if (score >= 80) return "TIGER"; // High Agency

if (score >= 50) return "CUB"; // Developing

return "MOUSE"; // Low Agency (Needs "Thud")

},



generateFeedback(score) {

if (score >= 80) return "🔥 EXCELLENT. HIGH VELOCITY. NO HESITATION.";

if (score >= 50) return "⚠️ GOOD. BUT STOP APOLOGIZING. LOUDER.";

return "🛑 STOP. YOU ARE WHISPERING. REPEAT WITH CHEST.";

}

};



module.exports = TIGER_ENGINE;

/**

* 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA — MOUSE-TO-TIGER ENGINE (STEP 024)

* LAW: DETECT WEAKNESS. INJECT POWER.

*/



const STATES = Object.freeze({

MOUSE: "MOUSE_MODE", // Hesitant, Apologetic (Tier 1)

CUB: "CUB_MODE", // Learning, Trying (Tier 2)

TIGER: "TIGER_MODE" // Sovereign, Direct (Tier 3 - 5M VND)

});



const MOUSE_TRIGGERS = [

"sorry", "bad english", "i think", "maybe", "xin loi", "uhm", "..."

];



const TIGER_TRIGGERS = [

"i will", "i want", "deploy", "execute", "money", "now"

];



function analyzeState(input, history) {

const text = input.toLowerCase();


// 1. DETECT MOUSE SIGNALS

let mouseScore = 0;

MOUSE_TRIGGERS.forEach(trigger => {

if (text.includes(trigger)) mouseScore++;

});



// 2. DETECT TIGER SIGNALS

let tigerScore = 0;

TIGER_TRIGGERS.forEach(trigger => {

if (text.includes(trigger)) tigerScore++;

});



// 3. DETERMINE STATE

let currentState = STATES.CUB; // Default


if (mouseScore > 0 && tigerScore === 0) {

currentState = STATES.MOUSE;

} else if (tigerScore > mouseScore) {

currentState = STATES.TIGER;

}



// 4. GENERATE FEEDBACK (THE ANTI-SHAME INTERVENTION)

let feedback = null;

if (currentState === STATES.MOUSE) {

feedback = "[ALERT] MOUSE VOICE DETECTED. STOP APOLOGIZING. SPEAK LOUDER.";

}



return {

state: currentState,

scores: { mouse: mouseScore, tiger: tigerScore },

intervention: feedback,

timestamp: Date.now()

};

}

[Source: 1748, 1792]

This logic parses the student's transcript (via Whisper or text input) and assigns a "Tiger Score."



/* =============================================================================

* STEP 023: TIGER CONFIDENCE SCORING ENGINE

* SOURCE: RAW-PHOENIX-LEDGER-2 [Source: 1592]

* ============================================================================= */



const PEDAGOGY_METRICS = {

// 🩸 PENALTY: "Grammar Prison" / Hesitation (-2 Points)

MOUSE_WORDS: [

"maybe", "think", "um", "sorry", "like", "kind of", "basically", "i guess"

],



// 🏆 REWARD: "Sovereign Action" (+3 Points)

TIGER_WORDS: [

"pivot", "navigate", "decide", "deliver", "build", "ship", "commit", "execute", "will"

],



calculateScore(transcript) {

let score = 50; // Base Confidence

const lower = transcript.toLowerCase();



// 1. Audit Mouse Drift

this.MOUSE_WORDS.forEach(word => {

const count = (lower.match(new RegExp(`\\b${word}\\b`, 'g')) || []).length;

score -= (count * 2);

});



// 2. Audit Tiger Velocity

this.TIGER_WORDS.forEach(word => {

const count = (lower.match(new RegExp(`\\b${word}\\b`, 'g')) || []).length;

score += (count * 3);

});



// 3. Clamp & Classify

score = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score));

return {

score: score,

tier: this.resolveTier(score)

};

},



resolveTier(score) {

if (score >= 80) return "TIGER"; // High Agency [Source: 1125]

if (score >= 41) return "CUB"; // Developing

return "MOUSE"; // Hesitant/Apologetic [Source: 1125]

}

};

/**

* ☢️ THE KENNETH PURGE V4.0

* MISSION: PROTECT THE MONOLITH FROM LOW VIBRATION.

* LAW: "If they whisper, we do not hear them."

*/



const KENNETH_FILTERS = {

// 🩸 THE MOUSE LEXICON (Instant Purge)

WEAK_SIGNALS: [

"maybe", "i think", "sorry", "plz", "can you",

"discount", "cheap", "free", "try"

],


// 💀 THE ENTROPY CHECK (Length/Effort)

MIN_CHARS: 10,


execute(requestBody) {

const text = (requestBody.content || "").toLowerCase();


// 1. EFFORT CHECK (The 2-Inch Stick Defense)

if (text.length < this.MIN_CHARS) {

throw new Error("[KENNETH_PURGE] SIGNAL_TOO_WEAK. REJECTED.");

}



// 2. VIBRATION CHECK (Mouse Detection)

const weakCount = this.WEAK_SIGNALS.filter(w => text.includes(w)).length;

if (weakCount >= 2) {

// Log the purge to the "Cold" ledger, but do not process.

console.warn(`[KENNETH_PURGE] VAPORIZED: ${text.substring(0, 20)}...`);

throw new Error("[KENNETH_PURGE] LOW_AGENCY_DETECTED. COME BACK AS A TIGER.");

}



// 3. BLUE CHECK (Gospel 444)

if (text.includes("blue") || text.includes("#0000ff")) {

throw new Error("[KENNETH_PURGE] VIOLATION: CORPORATE NOISE DETECTED.");

}



return "PASSED_V4";

}

};

/**

const TMST_LIMIT_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000; // 600,000ms (10 Minutes)



class Watchdog {

constructor(sessionId, context) {

this.sessionId = sessionId;

this.startTime = Date.now();

this.artifactsGenerated = 0;

this.valueCreated = false;

this.context = context;

}



logArtifact() {

this.artifactsGenerated++;

this.valueCreated = true;

this.lastActive = Date.now(); // Reset drift timer

}



audit() {

const elapsed = Date.now() - this.startTime;


// 1. THE KILL SWITCH

if (elapsed > TMST_LIMIT_MS && !this.valueCreated) {

return {

status: "FAIL",

action: "TERMINATE_SESSION",

reason: "[BLOAT_DETECTED] 10m elapsed with zero yield. Kill it."

};

}

return {

status: "WARNING",

action: "TRIGGER_URGENCY",

reason: "[DRIFT_RISK] 5m elapsed. Where is the artifact?"

};

}



return { status: "PASS", velocity: this.artifactsGenerated / (elapsed / 60000) };

}

}

function sha256(data) {
    return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(data).digest('hex');
}

    buildTree(nodes) {
        if (nodes.length === 0) return sha256("EMPTY_VOID_444");
        if (nodes.length === 1) return nodes[0];

        const nextLevel = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length; i += 2) {
            const left = nodes[i];
            const right = i + 1 < nodes.length ? nodes[i + 1] : left; // Duplicate odd leaf
            nextLevel.push(sha256(left + right));
        }
        return this.buildTree(nextLevel);
    }

    getRoot() {
        return this.root;
    }
}
